SUBJECT: one fictional adult Korean woman in her early twenties, with large round dark eyes, a small nose, coral-red lips, long side-parted black waves and small cubic-zirconia stud earrings. Keep the face established in the first shot consistent throughout. Visible pores and natural skin texture, without doll-like smoothness or a beauty filter.

OUTFIT: throughout all 30 seconds, she wears an indigo denim one-shoulder crop top and asymmetric-hem skirt, burgundy leather belt and pointed burgundy ankle boots. Keep the exposed shoulder, stitching and direction of the longer skirt panel consistent with their appearance in the first shot. Her outfit and hair do not change between locations.

CAMERA: the texture of a personally filmed phone travel video. She films the selfies. An off-screen travel companion films the two-handed chicken-cutlet handoff, her back in the market, and the restaurant food and eating shots. Changing operators does not introduce filming equipment or device UI into the image. Landscape 16:9, no black padding. Ordinary travel shots use a 26mm-equivalent phone wide angle, enough depth of field to read the surroundings, and natural iPhone colour. Handheld movement follows real footsteps; exposure and focus catch up slightly late. A fingerprint on the lens causes local light smearing beside strong light sources. No cinematic colour grade, studio lighting or added post-production glow. People in the surroundings move independently and do not stare into the lens for long.
EXCEPTIONS: only the three restaurant shots from 17 to 23 seconds use a DV-tape texture, light noise and slightly delayed focus. This deliberately differs from the travel footage; phone texture returns at 23 seconds. The food close-up at 19–21 seconds and the sunset focus change at 23–26.5 seconds follow their own shot-specific depth-of-field directions.

MUSIC: upbeat BGM carries the excitement and rhythm across the travel montage. Location sounds do not repeatedly interrupt the music. Only the gondola section has a one-beat drop. Dialogue is brief and relaxed, with audible breaths and complete word endings. Mandarin is limited to the opening and closing greetings, delivered as newly learned phrases. The ㅋㅋㅋ in the dialogue means laughter, not spoken letters.

PROPS: the bubble-tea clerk directly hands her a clear cup; the chicken-cutlet stall owner directly hands her the fried-chicken bag. In the xiaolongbao restaurant, the waiter sets the steamer with its lid on the table and she eats with chopsticks in her right hand and a spoon in her left. Food and containers are used only in their respective food scenes.

(0:00–0:02) — Guesthouse window, Taipei morning. She films a selfie from a low angle on her phone, smiling broadly. Tilt Up extends the frame above her face to the view through the window. Taipei 101's green-glass tower rises beyond low grey buildings, rooftop water tanks and air-con units. Exposure catches up with the bright sky. Dialogue: "你好~ 대만 왔다!" Transition: Hard cut on beat.

(0:02–0:04) — Main-road junction. At the crossing, she turns the phone into selfie position. A packed queue of helmeted scooter riders leaves the white stop line as the light turns green, passing behind her. Overcast daylight, Handheld Selfie and deep focus. Dialogue: "스쿠터 뭐야 미쳤어ㅋㅋㅋ" Transition: Fast cut on beat.

(0:04–0:06) — Bubble-tea counter. She points the phone in her left hand downward to film. Her right hand reaches out first. With arm and torso visible, the aproned clerk lowers the clear cup upright into it. After she supports its base and side, the clerk releases it. The sealed film lid faces up; black brown-sugar streaks line the cup. A wide straw rests on the counter. Insert, briefly Static. Transition: Snap cut.

(0:06–0:08) — Chicken-cutlet stall. The companion films from beside her, including both her hands and the owner. The large fried cutlet, seasoned with pepper and chilli, is already in a white paper bag. She extends both hands first. With arms and torso visible, the owner lowers the bag with its opening facing up. Only after her two hands support the bottom does the owner release it. Steam rises above the bag. Handheld Insert under a daylight awning. Transition: Cut at the end of the handoff.

(0:08–0:10) — Longshan Temple. She starts with a selfie and raises the phone. Tilt Up shifts the view from her face to incense smoke, red lanterns and the dragon-carved roof. The sky above the eaves is bright in natural daylight. Transition: Cut during the upward movement.

(0:10–0:12.5) — Jiufen stepped lane. She extends her arm high for a selfie and speaks. As she tilts the phone downward in a Tilt Down, her face leaves the frame and layers of red lanterns along the narrow stone stairway and wooden teahouse windows on both sides come into view. A misty afternoon; light fog veils the lower stairs. Dialogue: "여기 진짜 영화 속인데" Transition: Cut while looking down the stairway.

(0:12.5–0:15) — Dihua Street market. The companion films a waist-height Follow Shot from behind her. She raises the chicken-cutlet bag in one hand and takes a bite while walking through the passage. On either side of her indigo denim outfit, sacks of dried seafood, dried shiitake, tea tins, red envelopes and herbal-medicine drawers pass in sequence. Other people independently browse or walk by. Overcast afternoon light. Transition: Cut on forward movement.

(0:15–0:17) — Maokong gondola. In this scene she is not holding food or its bag. She films downward through the glass floor, holding the phone at knee height in one hand. Green tea fields drift beneath her feet and a cable shadow passes across them. Low Static. The music drops for one beat and returns. Transition: Snap cut.

(0:17–0:19) — Xiaolongbao restaurant, DV texture begins. She is already seated; the companion films a Static Medium from eye level across the table. The waiter sets a bamboo steamer upright on the table with its lid on, then lifts the lid. Six xiaolongbao on white paper, shredded ginger and black vinegar. She watches and speaks while the waiter performs the action. Dialogue: "이거 육즙 터진다며?" Transition: Cut to the open steamer.

(0:19–0:21) — Broth close-up. The companion films a downward-looking Macro from beside the table, with shallow depth of field. One xiaolongbao is already on the spoon in her left hand. She opens the skin slightly with the tips of the chopsticks in her right hand; broth flows into the spoon. Steam brushes past the lens. DV texture continues. Transition: Cut from the filled spoon.

(0:21–0:23) — First mouthful. The companion films a close Handheld shot from across the table. Begin immediately after her first mouthful of xiaolongbao. She lowers the spoon in her left hand and smiles toward the lens. White restaurant fluorescent light, DV texture continues. Dialogue: "미쳤다 진짜." Transition: Cut to sunset footage with phone texture.

(0:23–0:26.5) — Tamsui Fisherman's Wharf, signature. Phone texture returns. She walks beside the railing while filming an extended-arm selfie. The white Lover's Bridge and its reflection on the sea catch orange sunset light; her long waves and asymmetric denim hem move in the wind. Rack Focus: focus moves from the initially sharp bridge behind her to her face, leaving the bridge softly blurred. Actual sunset light, without added post-production glow. 60% slow motion. Transition: Cut when focus settles on her face.

(0:26.5–0:30) — Shifen railway, night. She films a selfie with the phone extended in her right hand. Her left hand releases the lower edge of a red sky lantern bearing brush calligraphy. The lit lantern leaves her fingertips and rises into the sky. Shop lights and other sky lanterns remain around her. She waves at the lens with her now-free left hand while speaking. Dialogue: "그럼 모두 再見~!" That left hand covers the lens, ending at 30 seconds. Natural night phone exposure.

CONSISTENCY: preserve the same face and single denim outfit. Contact between hands and props is visible, without smeared fingers. Do not add subtitles, graphic text, logos or watermarks to the footage. Writing that belongs to the location or props, including the lantern calligraphy, remains. No filming equipment is visible in frame.

FINALLY: skin and movement should look like real travel footage, not AI-generated imagery. Dialogue remains clear within natural breathing, without slurred syllables or clipped word endings. Expressions and gestures respond to the situation rather than posing like an advertising model. #DomoAI #DomoAICPP

```
FORMAT: 30 seconds | 16:9 | Ultra-photorealistic live-action | Continuous handheld vacation vlog

Preserve the exact face, hairstyle, identity, skin tone, facial features, and body proportions from <<<image_1>>> throughout the entire video. The same woman must appear consistently in every shot.

VISUAL STYLE: Authentic late-2000s personal vacation vlog recorded on a cheap handheld flip camera. Extremely raw and imperfect footage. Heavy natural camera shake, awkward handheld framing, occasional partial face crops, sudden reframing, autofocus hunting, exposure fluctuations, blown highlights, slightly soft image quality, warm faded colors, mild digital noise, compression artifacts, and subtle motion blur. No cinematic camera movements, no gimbal stabilization, no polished composition, no modern color grading, no beauty filter, no artificial sharpness.

Outfit: oversized grey hooded sweatshirt, black leggings, worn white running shoes, hair pulled back into a low messy ponytail, small silver stud earrings, a canvas tote bag slung over one shoulder. The entire video must feel like one genuine recording captured by a friend on an early-morning trip through a fishing village in Sokcho, South Korea, not a staged commercial or cinematic recreation.

00:00–00:04 — EARLY MORNING ARRIVAL
The woman walks along a quiet harbor path as the sun rises, fishing boats bobbing gently in the water behind her, seagulls circling overhead.
The camera shakes slightly as it follows her from the side.
She says:
"It's so early, but I really wanted to see the fish market before it gets busy."

00:04–00:08 — THE FISH MARKET
She steps into a covered harbor market filled with tanks of live fish, crabs, and squid, fishermen unloading crates from small boats nearby.
The camera zooms unsteadily on a tank of wriggling crabs, focus hunting before snapping back to her face.
She reacts with a mix of fascination and mild alarm.

00:08–00:12 — TALKING WITH A FISHERMAN
An older fisherman hoists a large fish onto a table for her to see up close.
She leans in curiously, asks a question, and he answers while gesturing with his hands.
She laughs and says to the camera:
"He says this one was caught just an hour ago!"

00:12–00:16 — TRYING RAW SQUID
A nearby stall vendor offers her a small plate of freshly sliced raw squid with dipping sauce.
She hesitates for a second, then takes a bite, chewing slowly with a surprised expression.
She turns to the camera and says:
"Okay wow, that's way fresher than I expected."

00:16–00:20 — HELPING SORT THE CATCH
A fisherwoman nearby is sorting fish into baskets and waves her over to help.
She kneels down and awkwardly tries to sort a few fish, nearly dropping one, laughing at herself.
The fisherwoman laughs too and pats her on the back encouragingly.

00:20–00:24 — WALKING THE HARBOR
She walks along the docks past rows of moored fishing boats, ropes coiled on the ground, nets drying in the morning sun.
A couple of fishermen nod and greet her; she waves back cheerfully.
The camera briefly swings toward the boats before swinging back to her.

00:24–00:27 — QUIET MOMENT
She stops at the edge of the harbor, looking out over the calm water as the sun fully rises over the horizon.
For a moment there's no dialogue — just waves lapping, distant gulls, and the creak of boats.
She smiles softly and says:
"Mornings like this make the early wake-up worth it."

00:27–00:30 — NATURAL ENDING
She turns back toward the camera, gives a small wave, and says:
"Okay, harbor adventure done. See you next time!"
She laughs, turns, and walks off along the dock.
The camera lingers a moment before the footage naturally ends with a slight shake and imperfect cut.

AUDIO
Natural location sound only:
- waves lapping against the dock
- seagulls calling
- boat engines idling in the distance
- fishermen calling out to each other
- crates being unloaded
- water splashing in fish tanks
- footsteps on wet pavement
- light morning breeze
```

Create a 30-second ultra-photorealistic Korean zombie survival thriller, cinematic live-action style.

CHARACTER CONSISTENCY: Young adult Korean woman, identical face, hairstyle, outfit, and body proportions to the reference image throughout. Expression evolves from terrified to desperate, determined, then shaken relief.

ZOMBIES: Aggressive, realistic human-like zombies with pale dirty skin, sunken eyes, damaged faces, messy hair, and torn everyday clothing. Practical horror makeup, no excessive gore.

LOCATION: One consistent dim Korean apartment tower: concrete stairwell, narrow numbered-door corridor, flickering greenish fluorescent lights, metal elevator with glowing floor indicator, CLOSE DOOR button, and pulsing red emergency light.

30-SECOND SEQUENCE (10 SHOTS):

1. 0–3s: Close-up, woman sprints down stairs as zombie hands reach from above.
2. 3–6s: She bursts into corridor, shoves an attacking zombie into the wall.
3. 6–9s: Rear tracking shot as she races toward the elevator, zombies emerging behind her.
4. 9–12s: She repeatedly presses elevator call button; horde approaches.
5. 12–15s: Doors open; she dives inside as zombies charge.

She has natural dark hair, minimal makeup, realistic skin texture, and believable proportions. Her face, hairstyle, lab coat, jeans, shoes, and body proportions must remain consistent throughout the entire sequence. Her terrified expression gradually disappears into a blank, emotionless stare as her eyes transform into an unnaturally deep obsidian black. She abruptly turns toward a nearby colleague and charges, forcing him behind a glass partition.

She repeatedly strikes the glass while staring directly at him, her completely black eyes and pulsing dark veins clearly visible. Two more scientists enter and freeze when she suddenly becomes perfectly still, then slowly rotates her head toward them. Around the laboratory, additional containment chambers begin cracking as warning lights activate. Another infected creature breaks free, sending the scientists scrambling for emergency equipment. They use a powerful emergency strobe and fire extinguisher to disorient the creatures before pushing a heavy steel cart across the corridor and retreating behind a security door.

The surviving scientists reach an emergency corridor and catch their breath as red warning lights wash over their exhausted faces. The sealed laboratory door violently shakes behind them. The camera gradually approaches its small observation window, revealing the infected woman standing motionless in the darkness, still wearing her white lab coat and blue jeans. Her pale face, prominent dark veins, and completely black eyes are visible through the glass. She slowly tilts her head toward the camera as the emergency lights flicker across her face. Cut to black.

Use handheld camera movement and natural diegetic sounds such as breaking glass, alarms, footsteps, breathing, impacts, convulsions, and distant screams. Avoid graphic gore or excessive blood. No anime, cartoon, illustration, artificial CGI appearance, plastic skin, duplicated characters, face morphing, distorted anatomy, floating objects, impossible movement, changing clothing, identity changes, subtitles, text overlays, logos, or watermark.

```
Create a 30-second ultra-realistic 4K live-action travel vlog featuring three young Korean female friends camping in the beautiful mountains of South Korea. Authentic handheld camcorder footage, natural friendship moments and realistic activities.

CHARACTERS:

- Main girl: Shoulder-length dark brown hair, beige fleece jacket, white T-shirt, olive cargo pants and hiking shoes.
- Friend 1: Black ponytail, dusty blue outdoor jacket, black pants and hiking shoes.
- Friend 2: Medium-length brown hair, brown knitted sweater, dark jeans and hiking shoes.

Keep all faces, hairstyles and outfits identical throughout.

EXACT 10-SCENE SEQUENCE — 30 SECONDS

1. ROAD TRIP (0–3s): A car travels along a winding Korean mountain road. The main girl films the forest from the passenger seat while her two friends laugh in the back.

2. CAMPSITE ARRIVAL (3–5s): They park at a grassy campsite surrounded by pine trees and mountains, then unload their tent, backpacks and supplies.

3. SETTING UP TENT (5–9s): They spread the tent fabric, connect poles, raise the tent, secure stakes and attach the rain cover. Show the complete assembly naturally.

4. CAMPSITE SETUP (9–11s): They arrange camping chairs, a folding table, backpacks, vegetables, meat and cooking equipment.

5. BARBECUE (11–15s): One girl prepares the portable grill, another chops vegetables, and the main girl grills marinated meat. Realistic sizzling, smoke and cooking movements.

6. DINNER (15–18s): They enjoy Korean barbecue, lettuce wraps, rice and side dishes while laughing and sharing food.

7. CAMPFIRE (18–22s): At night, they sit around a safe campfire. The main girl tells a funny story, her friends laugh, and warm firelight illuminates their faces.

8. STARRY NIGHT (22–24s): They admire the stars, pointing towards the sky while their tent glows softly behind them.

9. NEXT MORNING (24–26s): The main girl wakes inside her sleeping bag, stretches, unzips the tent and looks outside at the misty mountains.

10. SUNRISE ENDING (26–30s): All three walk to a safe mountain viewpoint overlooking misty green peaks. They admire the golden sunrise. The main girl smiles and waves at the camera. End with a beautiful wide handheld shot of the friends together.

VISUAL STYLE:
Ultra-photorealistic 4K travel vlog, authentic Korean mountains, natural handheld camera shake, imperfect framing, occasional autofocus adjustments, realistic lighting, skin textures, expressions, camping equipment, food, smoke, fire and morning mist.

AUDIO: Natural sounds only: car engine, forest wind, footsteps, tent assembly, sizzling barbecue, conversations, laughter, crackling fire, crickets and morning birds. No music or narration.

STRICT CONTINUITY:
Same three girls, outfits, campsite, tent and equipment throughout. Show every action in the correct order. Tent setup before cooking, cooking before eating, campfire after dinner and sunrise the following morning.

No teleportation, missing scenes, duplicated objects, distorted hands, sudden outfit changes, artificial CGI or unnatural movements.

FINAL GOAL: A warm, nostalgic and completely believable weekend camping vlog filled with genuine friendship, beautiful mountain scenery and a breathtaking sunrise. All 10 scenes must appear within exactly 30 seconds.
```

wet railway tracks, the approaching train becoming brighter as rain continues falling and its warm headlights reflect across the rails → cut back to the station as the train arrives and stops beside the platform, its warm interior lights glowing against the cold blue rainy atmosphere → final shot becomes a cinematic medium-wide shot of the young boy stepping into the warmly illuminated train while briefly looking back toward the tiny green creature near the bench, as rain continues falling outside and the train prepares to leave the quiet countryside station

<details>
<summary><b>Full prompt (50 lines, click to expand)</b></summary>

```
SEEDANCE 2.5 | TEXT TO VIDEO | SINGLE CONTINUOUS TAKE | 22s | 1080x1920 | 9:16 | 30fps | 1080p

SCENE AND LOCATION
A hidden volcanic spring canyon deep in a Pacific Northwest pine forest on a bright summer day. A narrow, deep gorge with steep, very high walls of dark gray basalt made of vertical hexagonal columns, streaked with tan and rust tones and covered in patches of bright green moss, ferns and small shrubs.
At the bottom is a crystal-clear natural spring pool. The shallow edges glow bright turquoise and aqua, fading to deep sapphire blue in the center. The water is so transparent that the bottom is fully visible: honeycomb-patterned volcanic rock, mossy boulders, and two long pale submerged logs lying diagonally across the bottom.
Two white waterfalls pour into the pool: a thin one at the far back of the canyon and a larger one spilling from the right-hand cliff wall, making white foam where it hits the water. Tall dark-green conifer and pine trees line the canyon rim. Above is a vivid clear blue sky with a few small white clouds. Hard, bright midday sunlight with strong contrast, and sun sparkles on the water.

THE JUMPER
A young man seen only from his own first-person view. Tanned bare feet and lightly hairy shins, and loose navy-blue swim shorts with a large mustard-yellow hibiscus flower pattern and white drawstrings. No shirt, no shoes, no gloves. His hands and forearms appear naturally in frame when he moves them.

CAMERA AND REALISM
First-person POV from a GoPro strapped to his forehead. It looks like real action-cam footage, not a cinematic camera. 16mm-equivalent ultra-wide lens with slight barrel distortion, and saturated but natural color.
Use real action-cam behavior throughout: small head bob and micro-jitter from breathing and body weight, rolling-shutter wobble on fast moves, quick auto-exposure shifts between bright sky and the shaded canyon, a small sun flare on upward tilts, light sensor noise in the shadows, and wind buffeting that shakes the frame during the fall.
The cliff is about 35 meters high. Gravity, body motion and water physics are fully realistic.

0-2s | THE EDGE (real time)
POV looking straight down from the top of the basalt cliff. His bare feet stand on the rough, dark rock ledge at the bottom of frame, with the shorts visible at the lower edge. Both hands hover forward at the sides of frame. The turquoise pool, the submerged logs and the waterfalls are far, far below. His toes curl over the edge and small grit crumbles off. His right hand tosses a pink-and-white striped inflatable tube out over the drop; it tumbles down and lands as a tiny speck on the water far below. Wind, distant waterfall roar, upbeat chill electronic music starts.

2-4s | THE COUNTDOWN (real time)
A quick nervous tilt down at the drop, a sharp exhale, then two fast steps back from the edge, with the ledge bouncing in frame. Young male voice, hyped: "Three, two, one!"

4-7s | THE TAKEOFF (natural slow motion, about 50% speed)
Two running steps forward, then his last foot pushes off the ledge in slow motion. Rock grit kicks up and floats in the sunlight, the cliff edge slides away beneath his feet, his arms swing forward and out wide, and the whole turquoise canyon opens up below. The slow motion looks like a real high-frame-rate action-cam clip, not a frozen hang. Deep bass swell, stretched wind.

7-13s | THE FALL (real time, fast and continuous)
The speed ramps smoothly back to normal and the stomach drops.
7-9s: The frame dips and wobbles, his arms flail slightly for balance at the edges of frame, and his feet kick at the bottom of frame. A loud yell: "Woooooo!"
9-11s: The basalt column wall, moss and ferns streak upward past the lens, and the right-side waterfall rushes by with spray catching the sun. Wind roars louder and the frame shakes from air buffeting.
11-13s: The glowing turquoise water rushes up and fills the frame, and the pink tube and submerged logs grow fast. His arms snap tight to his body and his legs lock straight for a feet-first pencil dive. The music builds to the drop.

13-14s | IMPACT
A hard feet-first hit into the water and a white explosion of bubbles swallows the lens. Loud plunge splash, deep impact whoosh, and the music goes heavily muffled underwater.

14-17s | UNDERWATER (short, energetic, real time)
A violent swirl of silver bubbles clears to reveal crystal-clear blue water, bright sun shafts cutting down from the surface, the pale submerged logs and honeycomb volcanic rock bottom below, and small fish darting away. His hands push into frame for one strong breaststroke pull, then fast kicks straight up toward the bright glowing surface, with bubbles streaming past the lens. No slow sinking.

17-19s | SURFACE BREAK
The camera bursts through the surface, water droplets sliding across the lens, and bright sunlight floods in. The music snaps back to full clarity on the beat drop. His hand grabs the pink-and-white tube floating right beside him.

19-22s | THE REWARD
POV bobbing on the turquoise water holding the tube, then a sharp tilt up revealing the towering basalt cliff, the tiny ledge he jumped from far above, the pine trees on the rim and the vivid blue sky, with the waterfall splashing on the right. Same voice, out of breath and triumphant: "Yeah, dude! That was insane!" Faint distant cheering. Music plays clean to the final frame.

AUDIO
Continuous upbeat chill electronic track: a bass swell on takeoff, a fast build through the fall, muffled underwater, then a clean drop on surfacing. Real ambient layers: wind that grows with speed, fabric flapping, heavy breathing, waterfall roar, splash, bubbles. Dialogue clear over the music.

KEEP
One continuous take, first-person only. The same shorts, feet and hands throughout, and the same canyon layout with two waterfalls, turquoise pool and submerged logs. The slow motion lasts only 3 seconds at takeoff. The fall is fast, real time and builds speed without pausing. Every beat is quick and punchy.

AVOID
Extra slow motion beyond the takeoff, hovering or floating mid-air, smooth cinematic gimbal movement, cuts to third person, showing the jumper's face, extra people, distorted hands or extra fingers, morphing cliffs, changing water color, text, subtitles, watermarks.
```

CAMERA: Begin low beside her feet → rapidly rise into a cinematic tracking orbit around her.

Camera follows directly through.

9–14 SEC — FUTURISTIC TOKYO

She steps through the same doorway directly into a futuristic Tokyo street at midnight.

Her walking movement continues seamlessly.

Wet streets reflect enormous neon signs, futuristic architecture and passing autonomous vehicles.

Crowds naturally move around her while she confidently walks toward camera.

CAMERA: 35mm anamorphic backward tracking shot → subtle handheld micro-movement → gentle push toward her face.

A sleek futuristic motorcycle suddenly races extremely close across the foreground.

For a fraction of a second, the motorcycle completely covers the camera.

USE THE MOTORCYCLE AS THE TRANSITION.

14–19 SEC — UNDERWATER BALLROOM

As the motorcycle clears the lens, she is suddenly walking through an enormous abandoned luxury ballroom deep underwater.

She does NOT wear diving equipment.

She walks normally while her dress and hair gently react to the surrounding water.

Gigantic floor-to-ceiling windows reveal the deep ocean outside.

A huge whale silently glides past the windows behind her.

Blue sunlight beams penetrate through the water while thousands of tiny particles float naturally through the scene.

CAMERA: Slow cinematic 180° orbit around her as she turns slightly toward the whale.

Suddenly hundreds of shimmering silver fish rush directly toward and across the camera.

They completely fill the frame.

19–23 SEC — GOLDEN DESERT

The silver fish seamlessly transform into thousands of glowing golden particles carried by desert wind.

Camera pushes through the particles.

Reveal my model walking along the crest of an enormous golden sand dune during sunset.

Her outfit has seamlessly transformed into an elegant flowing ivory desert outfit.

HER FACE AND IDENTITY MUST REMAIN EXACTLY THE SAME.

Wind dramatically moves the fabric.

CAMERA: Start extremely wide → rapidly fly toward her → smooth orbit around her body.

Reveal one final mysterious door standing alone at the top of the dune.

She opens it.

Brilliant golden-white sunlight bursts through.

23–28 SEC — ABOVE THE CLOUDS

She steps through the door.

Camera follows immediately.

Reveal an extraordinary mirrored.
#AICinema #Seedance25

CHARACTER:
A young woman wearing a cute, stylish amusement-park outfit with a clearly visible Mickey Mouse ears headband. Keep the headband consistent throughout the entire video. She is excited, playful, and expressive.
A cheerful Turkish ice cream vendor stands behind a traditional Turkish ice cream stall, holding a long metal ice cream paddle.

0–3 SEC — ORDER
Start with a cinematic wide shot of the colorful amusement park, then smoothly move toward the Turkish ice cream stall.

The girl approaches the counter, smiling excitedly. Her Mickey Mouse ears headband is clearly visible. She points toward the ice cream.
The vendor prepares a cone with a scoop of ice cream and extends it toward her.

3–6 SEC — THE FIRST TEASE
Cut to a medium shot.

The girl eagerly reaches for the cone.

He smiles mischievously and offers it again.

6–9 SEC — MORE TEASING
She reaches again.

The vendor smoothly moves the cone from left to right, then slightly upward, always just out of reach.

The girl follows the cone, laughing and becoming increasingly determined.

9–11 SEC — THE NOSE TRICK
The vendor finally brings the ice cream toward her.

She confidently leans forward, expecting to receive it.

Instead, he gently places a small dab of ice cream on the tip of her nose.

She pauses, looks cross-eyed toward the ice cream on her nose, then looks back at the vendor with a playful annoyed expression.
11–15 SEC — SHE WINS
Before the vendor can tease her again, she quickly grabs the cone from the paddle.

She successfully gets the ice cream.

The vendor laughs and raises his hands in playful defeat.

She proudly holds up the cone, smiles toward the camera, wipes the ice cream from her nose, and takes a happy bite.

CINEMATOGRAPHY:
Photorealistic live-action, premium cinematic quality, warm golden-hour lighting, natural skin texture, realistic facial expressions, shallow depth of field, soft background bokeh, subtle anamorphic lens characteristics, natural motion blur, smooth gimbal movement, cinematic push-ins and tracking shots, realistic amusement-park atmosphere.

IMPORTANT:

THE MIDNIGHT FASHION EDIT

15-second seamless looping fashion reel | Vertical 9:16 | 24 FPS | 5 scenes × 3 seconds

SEAMLESS OPENING & LOOP DESIGN

CRITICAL: The opening and ending must be visually identical and designed as one continuous camera movement.

OPENING FRAME (0.0s)

Begin with an extreme low-angle shot from behind the emerald-green velvet chaise lounge. A dark, out-of-focus foreground object partially covers the lens. Only a small portion of the golden lamp and warm penthouse lighting is visible.

The camera begins moving upward immediately, revealing the room as it rises toward the overhead composition.

ENDING FRAME (14.5–15.0s)

During the final clockwise barrel roll, rush toward the exact same dark foreground object used in the opening shot.

The foreground object gradually covers the entire lens until the final frame becomes completely dark.

Match the final frame to the opening frame in:

Camera position and height.
Lens angle and perspective.
Foreground object placement.
Darkness, exposure, and color.
Motion direction and speed.
Background lighting and visual composition.

LOOP CONNECTION

At exactly 15.0 seconds, the camera must continue the same upward movement established at 0.0 seconds. The opening rise should feel like the natural continuation of the final transition.

No fade-to-black followed by a separate restart. No sudden camera reset. No visible jump cut. No change in lighting.

The first and last frames must match precisely, and the motion across the cut must feel continuous.

FIVE FASHION SCENES

0–3s — THE CLASSIC MUSE

A timeless luxury outfit. She lies diagonally across the emerald velvet chaise, holding a perfume bottle above her face, then gently turns toward the camera.

3–6s — THE STREET-STYLE ICON

A completely different modern fashion look. One arm behind her head, the other near her waist. She adjusts her sleeve with a relaxed, confident expression.

6–9s — THE SOFT LUXURY LOOK

A romantic, elegant fashion look. She gently brushes her hair back and looks toward the overhead camera with a subtle smile.

9–12s — THE NIGHT OUT EDIT

A bold evening look. She holds an open fashion magazine above her chest, turns one page, and lowers it slightly.

12–15s — THE FINAL STATEMENT

A striking statement-fashion look. She rests one hand near her face, crosses her ankles, and gives the camera a subtle confident smile. The camera then performs the final clockwise barrel roll toward the dark foreground object.

ENVIRONMENT & CINEMATOGRAPHY

Maintain the same luxurious Parisian penthouse throughout: emerald-green velvet chaise, marble flooring, glowing city skyline, golden sculptural lamps, flowers, fashion magazines, vintage mirror, and candlelight.

Warm cinematic lighting, realistic fabric movement, subtle film grain, rich shadows, premium editorial color grading.

AUDIO

130 BPM laid-back luxury deep-house groove.
Consistent rhythm across all 15 seconds.
Bass impact and airy whoosh on each transition.
Natural fabric rustles and scene-specific foley.
No dialogue, no vocals.

FINAL TECHNICAL REQUIREMENTS

Duration: exactly 15 seconds.
Frame rate: 24 FPS.
Aspect ratio: 9:16.
Five scenes, exactly 3 seconds each.
Continuous visual environment.
Smooth camera movement.
No black-frame pause at the loop point.
No captions, logos, or watermarks.
Photorealistic live-action quality.

MOST IMPORTANT: Design the reel as a continuous circular camera journey. The final camera movement must return to the precise opening composition so viewers cannot tell where the video ends and begins. The loop must feel natural even when replayed repeatedly.
```

FANGS OF THE EMERALD DUSK

[ENGINE DIRECTIVE]
Create an exactly 30-second, 16:9 photorealistic Hollywood creature-action film. 60fps where supported, realistic textures, cinematic lighting, volumetric mist and natural motion blur.

CHARACTER LOCK: @image1 is the human hero. Preserve his exact face, skin tone, hair, beard, body, black outfit and props. @image2 is his werewolf form; match its fur, muzzle, eyes, ears, claws and proportions exactly in every shot. No human features or clothing remain in werewolf form. One consistent creature, no identity drift. Antagonist: a 4-meter muscular jungle behemoth with moss-covered bark armor, cracked antler-like skull crest, curved tusks and massive arms.

LOCATION: Rain-soaked ancient jungle clearing at dusk, giant buttress-root trees, hanging vines, fallen mossy log on the left, rocky muddy center and golden light shafts through mist. Fixed geography; hero enters from left, behemoth from right. Broken trees and environmental damage persist.

[30-SECOND TIMELINE]

00–05s | STANDOFF: Wide establishing shot, hero left and behemoth right, 30 meters apart. Boot and eye close-ups, beast eye close-up, chest heaving. They roar and sprint toward each other, splashing mud.

05–10s | TRANSFORMATION: Fast tracking shot. Both leap. In dramatic slow motion, the hero transforms completely into @image2: bones shift, muscles expand, fur erupts, face forms a wolf muzzle, claws extend, clothes vanish. They collide mid-air, blasting mist and leaves outward, then crash into mud.

10–15s | BRAWL: Werewolf claws the beast's shoulder and dodges its swing. The beast backhands him into a tree, cracking the trunk. The werewolf rebounds, lunges with both claws, and the beast slams the ground, spraying mud.

15–20s | REVERSAL: The beast pins the werewolf and thrusts its tusks downward. Werewolf catches its jaw, braces against its chest and kicks it into the fallen log. He rises and roars.

20–25s | FINAL COMBO: The beast charges. Werewolf runs up the broken leaning trunk, leaps over it, slashes its back mid-spin, then lands a knee strike and spinning kick. The beast staggers, knees buckling.

25–30s | FINISH: Slow motion: werewolf leaps and smashes both fists onto the beast's skull, driving it into cracked mud. The beast collapses motionless. Werewolf breathes heavily; in close-up, fur recedes, muzzle shortens, and he becomes exactly @image1 with original outfit restored. He smiles calmly and walks into golden mist. Hard cut at 30 seconds.

AUDIO: Original tribal orchestral score, war drums, low strings, taiko percussion, choir swell during transformation, synchronized bone cracks, fabric tearing, claws, roars, splintering wood and heavy impacts. Brief silence before final blow, thunderous kill impact, then warm strings and rain fading at exactly 30 seconds.

<details>
<summary><b>Full prompt (80 lines, click to expand)</b></summary>

```
Create a 30-second ultra-photorealistic Hollywood-level cinematic thriller scene titled “THE LAST CALL.” The entire sequence must feel like a scene from a high-budget live-action feature film, with realistic human acting, natural facial expressions, physically accurate movement, realistic rain, practical lighting, believable vehicle physics, detailed environments, cinematic depth of field, subtle film grain, natural skin texture, realistic reflections, and professional Hollywood cinematography. No anime, no CGI-looking visuals, no fantasy aesthetic, no cyberpunk, no neon lighting, no exaggerated visual effects.
MAIN CHARACTER: A realistic young woman in her late 20s, natural appearance, expressive eyes, shoulder-length dark hair slightly damp from the humid weather, wearing a simple dark jacket. Maintain the exact same woman, face, hairstyle, clothing, car, weather and environment consistently throughout the entire sequence.

SETTING: A remote mountain road at night during heavy but realistic rainfall. Dark forested mountains surround the narrow winding road. Wet asphalt reflects only natural headlights and road markings. The atmosphere is tense, isolated and believable.

0–5 SECONDS

Interior of a moving car at night. Medium shot from the passenger side showing the woman driving alone. Rain continuously hits the windshield and realistic windshield wipers move back and forth. Dashboard illumination softly lights her face. She looks tired and emotionally distant while concentrating on the road. Camera slowly pushes toward her face. Natural breathing, subtle blinking and realistic micro-expressions.

5–10 SECONDS

Her smartphone suddenly begins ringing in the center console. Cut to an extremely realistic close-up of the phone screen.

Caller ID: “MOM”

The woman freezes.

Her eyes widen slightly. Her hands tighten around the steering wheel. She looks at the phone in disbelief. She hesitates for a moment, then reaches down and answers the call.

10–17 SECONDS

Extreme close-up of her face as she listens.

A faint, emotional female voice comes through the phone:

“Don't take the next turn.”

The woman's expression changes from confusion to fear.

The call suddenly disconnects.

She looks at the phone.

The screen reads: CALL ENDED

Outside the windshield, a sharp mountain turn approaches through the heavy rain.

17–23 SECONDS

She immediately slows the car.

Camera moves to a forward-facing perspective through the windshield. The road curves sharply ahead.

Suddenly, distant headlights appear through the rain around the corner.

The woman looks toward them.

A large truck suddenly loses control and crashes through the roadside barrier at the exact location where her car would have been if she had continued driving.

The crash must look physically realistic and grounded, with believable vehicle momentum, rain, debris and impact. No exaggerated explosions.

23–27 SECONDS

Silence after the crash.

The woman sits completely still, breathing heavily, visibly shaken.

She slowly looks down at her phone.

The phone screen now shows:

NO SIM — NO SIGNAL

She looks confused and terrified.

27–30 SECONDS

Extreme close-up of her eyes filling with tears.

She looks toward the empty passenger seat.

A subtle emotional pause.

In a trembling whisper, she says:

“Mom?”

Cut instantly to black.

FINAL AUDIO: Heavy rain, windshield wipers, realistic car interior ambience, distant thunder, phone ringing, muffled female voice, sudden silence after the crash, then only the woman's breathing.
```

```
Create a 30-second, 16:9 premium live-action vampire-romance teaser for an international short-drama audience. Use Image 1 for the two leads' appearance and Image 2 for the castle environment. Preserve both identities consistently throughout every shot with ultra-realistic live-action cinematography, cinematic lighting, natural skin texture, and emotionally expressive performances.

Story: A curious young woman accidentally wanders into the forbidden wing of a forgotten castle, where she awakens an ancient vampire noble from centuries of sleep. The instant he sees her, he senses the bloodline of an ancient war flowing through her veins. Instead of killing her, he becomes dangerously fascinated. His obsession grows immediately into a possessive desire to keep her close. She is frightened by his overwhelming presence but refuses to surrender completely, meeting his dominance with quiet courage.

Style: Premium ReelShort / DramaBox vampire romance. Darkly romantic, emotionally intense, fate-bound, mysterious, elegant, and seductive without becoming explicit. Build escalating romantic tension through eye contact, silence between lines, restrained emotion, and powerful chemistry. The pacing should feel like the opening episode of a viral international drama with a shocking cliffhanger ending. No gore, no cheap horror, no Halloween costumes, no monsters, and no modern streetwear.

The castle is illuminated only by moonlight filtering through towering stained-glass windows, flickering candlelight, fireplaces, drifting mist, ancient chandeliers, marble floors, velvet curtains, towering bookshelves, gothic arches, carved stone walls, and floating dust particles. Rich black, crimson, silver, and deep blue dominate the color palette.

Shoot entirely for TikTok, ReelShort, and DramaBox. Prioritize medium close-ups, close-ups, extreme close-ups, slow push-ins, over-the-shoulder shots, dramatic eye contact, trembling hands, subtle breathing, and intimate framing. Every shot should maximize emotional tension between the leads.

Scene 1 (0–5s)
The young woman cautiously walks through the abandoned castle corridor holding an antique candle. The flame flickers violently as if something unseen is watching her.

Woman (whispering):
"I shouldn't be here..."

A distant male voice echoes through the darkness.

Vampire (O.S., whisper):
"...You finally came."

Scene 2 (5–10s)
An enormous stone coffin slowly opens. The vampire noble rises with quiet elegance, his crimson eyes fixed only on her. She freezes in fear.

Woman:
"Who... who are you?"

Vampire (calmly):
"The one fate refused to bury."

Scene 3 (10–15s)
He slowly approaches until only inches separate them. He gently lifts her chin without hurting her, studying her face as though recognizing someone long forgotten.

Vampire:
"Your blood remembers a war the world has forgotten."

Woman:
"I don't know what you're talking about."

Scene 4 (15–20s)
She steps backward, refusing to surrender despite her fear. He blocks her escape with effortless confidence.

Woman:
"I'm not afraid of you."

Vampire (soft smile):
"You should be."

Scene 5 (20–25s)
He leans closer, eyes locked with hers. Their breathing becomes the only sound. His expression shifts from curiosity to obsession.

Vampire:
"I searched centuries for you..."

Woman (barely whispering):
"Why me?"

Vampire:
"Because you belong to my destiny."

Scene 6 (25–30s)
The castle doors slam shut by themselves. Candle flames extinguish one by one. He gently reaches for her hand while she hesitates, torn between fear and an irresistible pull.

Vampire:
"You may fight fate..."

(He slowly intertwines his fingers with hers.)

Vampire:
"...but you'll never escape me."

Final shot: Extreme close-up of their locked eyes as orchestral music swells. Smash cut to black before they kiss.

Editing should feel premium and fast-paced with cinematic hard cuts, dramatic push-ins, elegant slow motion, realistic sound design, orchestral strings, deep piano notes, distant thunder, candle crackles, footsteps, soft breathing, fabric movement, and subtle heartbeats. The final frame ends on a cliffhanger that leaves the audience desperate for Episode 2.
```

THE DRAGON WARRIOR — GOLDEN HARBOR AMBUSH

ENGINE DIRECTIVE: Create an exactly 30-second, 16:9, photorealistic Hollywood martial-arts action sequence, targeting 60 FPS. Use @image1 as protagonist identity, @image2 as ninja antagonist and @image3 as harbor environment.

CHARACTER CONTINUITY:
@image1: Preserve exact face, beard, hairstyle, skin tone and body proportions. Outfit: deep-teal martial-arts tunic, black leather armor with embossed golden dragon emblem, black shoulder guards and bracers, teal trousers, black combat boots and traditional conical bamboo hat. Bare-handed fighter.

@image2: Eight masked black-clad ninja assassins. Their leader wears layered charcoal shinobi robes, black leather armor, reinforced guards, cross-body straps, black mask and conical hat. He carries a curved steel katana with black handle and scabbard. Maintain identical appearances.

@image3: Ancient wooden harbor surrounded by misty mountains, traditional sailing ships, beige sails, turquoise dock posts, cargo crates and wooden market stalls. Golden sunrise, atmospheric mist and reflective water. Maintain exact geography.

AUDIO: Original Asian cinematic action music with bamboo flute, strings and powerful drums. Add synchronized punches, kicks, sword swishes, footsteps, impacts, harbor ambience and ship creaks.

TIMELINE

00:00–00:03 | AMBUSH
Wide harbor reveal. Warrior walks along the narrow pier as masked ninjas emerge. Low-angle cinematic tracking.

00:03–00:06 | FIRST ATTACK
A ninja charges. Warrior blocks with his forearm and punches the attacker in the ribs, knocking him down.

00:06–00:09 | SIDE KICK
Second ninja attacks. Warrior delivers a powerful side kick, sending him backward.

00:09–00:12 | SURROUNDED
Three ninjas attack together. Warrior catches a leaping enemy's leg and throws him onto the wooden pier.

00:12–00:15 | SWORDS DRAWN
Camera pushes toward the wider cargo platform. Remaining ninjas draw curved swords. Leader reveals his katana.

00:15–00:18 | BLADE COMBAT
Leader swings at the warrior. He ducks, controls the weapon wrist and punches the attacker in the torso.

00:18–00:21 | COUNTERATTACK
Another swordsman attacks from behind. Warrior pivots, avoids the blade and throws him against a cargo crate.

00:21–00:24 | FINAL THROW
Leader charges again. Warrior redirects his sword arm and performs a powerful rotational hip throw. Katana slides across the deck.

00:24–00:27 | VICTORY
Wide shot of unconscious ninjas across the wooden platform. Warrior stands confidently against the golden sunrise.

00:27–00:30 | FINAL STANCE
Slow cinematic push-in. Warrior enters a low martial-arts stance, looks toward camera and says: "Watch out for this one." Dramatic drum impact, fade to black at exactly 30 seconds.

CONTINUITY: Preserve all reference identities, costumes, weapons and environment. Realistic martial-arts physics, natural movement, cinematic lighting and consistent geography. No morphing, teleportation, duplicated limbs, gore, cartoon visuals, subtitles or watermarks.
```

<details>

```
Perfect AI cinematography is overrated.
Sometimes you need shaky tracking, missed framing, ugly zooms and compression artifacts to make something feel REAL. 👀

This Seedance 2.5 experiment proves it.
Full prompt below 👇
Ultra-realistic viral helicopter pursuit footage, raw local-news style, filmed entirely from one helicopter in bright Los Angeles daylight. Long-lens aerial camera, unstable vibration, abrupt zooms, compression artifacts, heat haze, autofocus breathing, imperfect framing, rotor thump, distant radio chatter, harsh California sun, no cinematic polish, believable amateur-news realism.

Main subject: one motocross rider on a dirt bike, full helmet and motocross gear, highly skilled, aggressive but controlled riding. Keep the same rider and same bike throughout.

Los Angeles daytime: palm-lined boulevard, parking lots, freeway ramps, concrete flood channel, industrial blocks, overpasses, service roads and distant skyline.

0-5s: Immediate viral hook The helicopter camera is already searching frantically when the rider suddenly blasts into frame between moving cars. Operator snaps into a shaky zoom. The rider jumps a curb, cuts across a parking lot and nearly disappears behind palm trees.

5-10s: Chaotic tracking He rejoins a boulevard, threads through traffic, then veers up a sloped driveway and launches over a low divider. Camera over-zooms, loses him for half a second, then catches him landing cleanly.

10-15s: Escalation The rider enters a concrete flood channel. Huge aerial reveal. He accelerates along the channel floor while the helicopter banks hard overhead. Dust trails behind the bike. Quick zoom on spinning rear wheel and suspension.

15-20s: Viral stunt beat He rides up the angled concrete wall, holds a dramatic high line for a moment, then drops back down smoothly. Camera shakes violently trying to follow. Rotor vibration and radio chatter intensify.

20-25s: Big surprise The rider exits through a service ramp, crosses beneath an overpass, then unexpectedly appears on a parallel industrial road. The operator pans late and barely recovers him. He hits a natural rise and gets long airtime in full daylight.

25-30s: Iconic ending He lands, accelerates toward another overpass and vanishes into shadow underneath. The helicopter overshoots, circles, zooms wildly across multiple roads, but he is gone. Reporter voice, stunned: “We just lost him.” Hold on shaky wide aerial of Los Angeles. Hard cut.

One continuous helicopter perspective, strong amateur-news authenticity, same rider throughout, realistic motocross physics, believable Los Angeles geography, harsh daylight, imperfect tracking, viral unpredictability, readable stunts, no crash, no gore, no readable text, no logos.

#DreaminaCPP  @dreamina_ai
```

</details>

<details>

```
TITLE: "What Falls from the Plum Tree?"
DURATION: 15 seconds | ASPECT RATIO: 16:9
GENRE: Romantic Japanese Fantasy Comedy
STYLE: Ultra-realistic premium Japanese drama, cinematic film quality

IDENTITY LOCK:
The woman @image1 must keep the exact same face, face shape, skin tone, hairstyle, height, body proportions, and wardrobe in every shot. The man @image2 must keep his exact face, hairstyle, and outfit as in the reference. No changes in any frame.

ENVIRONMENT LOCK:
An elegant Edo-era Japanese shrine courtyard with traditional wooden architecture, tiled rooftops, stone lanterns, and a raked gravel garden in the background. A giant ancient plum (ume) tree with dense white and pink blossoms. Its thick main branch extends horizontally above the woman's head. Soft spring daylight.

SHOT BREAKDOWN:

0.0–4.0s | Medium shot
The woman stands beneath the huge horizontal branch. Blossom petals drift in the breeze. She looks up curiously, then raises both hands and shakes the branch. The branch trembles and a shower of petals falls. A lacquered cosmetic box tumbles down through the blossoms and she catches it with both hands. Close-up of her face as she peeks inside. Flat, unimpressed expression. She tosses it casually out of frame.

4.0–7.5s | Medium shot
Her curiosity grows. She shakes the branch harder and petals pour down like rain. A scatter of gold koban coins and a few silk pouches fall. She catches one pouch, raises a single eyebrow, looks bored, and tosses it away.

7.5–10.5s | Slow motion
Determined now, she grips the branch with both hands and shakes with all her strength. The branch shudders, a heavy blossom storm rains down, and the man @image2 suddenly drops from the branch. She gasps, spreads her arms, and catches him just before he hits the ground. Petals swirl around them.

10.5–13.0s | Close-up, 50mm
She looks at his face. Her eyes sparkle and a wide, delighted smile appears. He gives a charming, slightly embarrassed smile and looks back at her.

13.0–15.0s | Medium-wide shot
They turn to the camera together and make small heart shapes with their hands, laughing happily. They turn to each other, laugh, and walk out of frame side by side while petals keep falling behind them. Fade out on the falling blossoms.

EMOTION ARC:
Curious → Bored → More curious → Surprised → Smitten → Happy.

SOUND:
Soft spring breeze, rustling blossoms, creaking wood, light thud of the box, jingle of coins, warm playful Japanese rom-com music (koto, light shamisen, soft piano), cheerful laughter, fading footsteps.

CAMERA & VISUALS:
ARRI Alexa 35 look, 35mm and 50mm cinematic lenses, smooth gimbal movement, slow-motion petal shower, natural spring lighting, realistic skin pores, physically accurate shadows, HDR, subtle film grain, soft pastel color grading.

NEGATIVE PROMPT:
cartoon, anime, CGI look, low quality, blurry face, extra fingers, warped hands, duplicate objects, duplicate people, distorted body, floating objects, unrealistic physics, unnatural falling motion, overexposed lighting, oversaturated colors, AI artifacts, camera shake, abrupt cuts, warped architecture, deformed eyes

It is late evening during sunset. The sun is slowly disappearing behind the rooftops, creating a deep orange, muted golden and dark purple sky. Long shadows stretch across the gardens and streets

21–26 SECONDS:

She quickly turns towards her entrance. Through the narrow entrance hallway, she notices zombies approaching outside her front door. She rushes towards the already-closed front door and checks the lock, urgently securing it again. Her movement must remain physically believable and anatomically correct.
26–30 SECONDS:

She steps backwards and leans against the locked door, breathing heavily. Close-up of her frightened face. In the background, faint zombie footsteps and distant unsettling noises. Suddenly, three heavy knocks hit the front door. Cut instantly to black.
VISUAL QUALITY:

Photorealistic Unreal Engine 5 style graphics, premium AAA survival horror game cinematography, realistic human anatomy, lifelike facial expressions, physically accurate character movement, realistic zombie walking animation, cinematic depth of field, volumetric sunset lighting, atmospheric shadows, detailed English architecture, professional camera work, subtle film grain, realistic environmental physics.
CAMERA:

Smooth controlled cinematic tracking shots, realistic handheld camera for suspense, wide establishing shots of the neighbourhood, close-up facial reactions and carefully composed point-of-view shots.
STRICT NEGATIVE INSTRUCTIONS:

No cartoon visuals, no anime, no artificial-looking CGI, no exaggerated facial expressions, no unnatural body movements, no distorted anatomy, no extra limbs, no floating objects, no teleporting zombies, no sliding feet, no robotic walking.

ROMAN GLADIATOR: FIGHT FOR GLORY

Duration: 30 seconds | Format: 16:9, 24 FPS | Style: Photorealistic Hollywood cinema, ARRI ALEXA LF, anamorphic lenses, realistic physics, natural skin texture, fine film grain.

SCENE: Ancient Roman amphitheater at golden hour, packed with 50,000 cheering spectators. A king and queen watch from a shaded imperial box. Golden sunlight comes consistently from screen-left, illuminating dusty sand, bronze armor, and flying particles.

CHARACTERS:

- Main character (@image1): Preserve 100% the reference face, hairstyle, skin tone, and athletic build. Dark-red wool tunic, leather chest harness, bronze shoulder guard, bracers, greaves, sandals, gladius in right hand, round bronze shield on left arm.
- ENEMY: One-and-a-half heads taller, heavily muscled, scarred torso, polished bronze helmet with crimson crest, iron arm guard, broad sword, rectangular wooden shield with faded red emblem.
- KING & QUEEN: King with grey-streaked beard, laurel wreath, purple-and-gold toga. Queen with dark braided updo, ivory-and-gold stola, gold jewelry.

TIMED MULTISHOT — SIX SHOTS, HARD CUTS ONLY AT 5, 10, 15, 20, AND 25 SECONDS.

SHOT 1 | 0–5s | REAL-TIME: High crane descends into the arena. The king raises his hand, then signals the duel with a horn blast. The hero and enemy raise their shields and approach from opposite sides.

SHOT 2 | 5–10s | 2× SPEED: Ground-level handheld orbit. Both warriors charge and clash swords, producing sparks. The hero parries, ducks, sidesteps, and counters. The enemy lands a powerful shield bash, forcing the hero backward.

SHOT 3 | 10–15s | 40% SLOW MOTION: The enemy smashes the hero's shield, splintering it. The hero drops his gladius and falls onto the sand. The enemy steps over him and raises his sword for the finishing strike. Rack focus from the fallen weapon to the hero's determined face.

SHOT 4 | 15–20s | REAL-TIME: The enemy swings downward. The hero catches his forearm, stopping the blade inches from his throat. He strikes the enemy's ribs with an elbow, headbutts the bronze visor, then punches him hard. The enemy staggers backward and drops his shield.

SHOT 5 | 20–25s | 40% SLOW MOTION, CLIMAX: The hero retrieves his gladius, rises, and charges. He slides beneath the enemy's horizontal sword swing, slashes across his torso, and drives his shoulder into the enemy's chest. The giant flies backward three meters, his sword spinning away, then crashes onto the sand in a dramatic dust explosion.

SHOT 6 | 25–30s | REAL-TIME: The exhausted, bloodied hero stands over the motionless enemy and raises his gladius toward the imperial box, roaring triumphantly. The king rises in approval, the queen smiles, and the camera cranes upward and pulls back to reveal the roaring amphitheater.

CAMERA & PHYSICS: Distinct cinematic framing for every shot, controlled camera movement, realistic sword impacts, natural body weight, flying sparks, falling wood splinters, authentic sand displacement, consistent contact shadows, stable faces and anatomy.

LIGHTING & COLOR: Low golden sun from screen-left, bronze highlights, ochre sand, deep blue shadows, crimson accents, purple royal toga, volumetric dust, cinematic HDR and filmic highlights.

AUDIO: Continuous crowd roar, horn blast, metallic sword clashes, shield impacts, grunts, accelerating taiko percussion, dramatic strings and choir at the climax, triumphant brass ending.

CONTINUITY LOCKS: @image1 remains identical in every shot. Maintain the same outfits, faces, weapons, lighting direction, and arena layout. Shield intact until 10s, broken afterward. Enemy's shield drops by 20s; he retains his helmet and sword until the climax, then lies motionless from 25–30s. Only two fighters on the arena floor. Every shot fills its full five seconds with one consistent playback speed.

**00:20–00:23** Over-the-shoulder, 50mm Steadicam. He leans out of the window and aims at the SUV's front tire, with a single muzzle flash.

**00:23–00:26** SLOW MOTION, low side angle, 35mm. The tire bursts and the SUV flips and rolls across the highway in sparks and dust, fully visible. Mercedes sharp in the foreground.

**00:26–00:28** Close-up, 85mm, slow push-in. @image1 hero gives a slow, confident victory smile and a small nod.

**00:28–00:30** High-angle drone rise, 24mm. The Mercedes speeds toward the sunset, smoke rising from the wreck far behind. Fade out.

The floor is completely flooded with ankle-to-knee-deep dirty rainwater, creating strong reflections and splashes everywhere. Use realistic physics, natural human movement, detailed wet clothing, wet hair sticking to her face, realistic water droplets, splashes, reflections and motion blur. The camera should move dynamically with fast tracking shots, low-angle shots, side tracking shots and occasional wide shots revealing the scale of the flooding.

At one point, show the woman jumping over obstacles and running across a market counter/raised surface while the floodwater continues rushing behind her. Keep her appearance, hairstyle, pink outfit and facial features consistent throughout the entire sequence.

Dark cinematic atmosphere, overcast rainy lighting, cool blue-gray tones mixed with warm fluorescent market lights, dramatic contrast, realistic skin texture, photorealistic environment, volumetric lighting, atmospheric mist, cinematic depth of field, subtle film grain, high-detail textures, realistic reflections and physically accurate water simulation.

The sequence should feel like a high-budget survival/action movie, extremely immersive and intense, with realistic camera shake and fast-paced movement.

Add large cinematic white brush-stroke typography reading “RUN” briefly across the center of the screen during the action sequence.

Photorealistic, ultra-detailed, cinematic Hollywood-style action scene, realistic physics, natural motion, 4K film quality, anamorphic lens look, dynamic composition, dramatic pacing, no cartoon look, no CGI appearance, no distorted anatomy, no extra fingers, no duplicated people, no face changes.
